# M05 - Adjudication, acceptance, and correction routing

## Module contract and selection

| Module type | Decision | Instance IDs | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| M05 | SELECTED | MI-NORMAL-001-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-001-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-001-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-001-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-002-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-002-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-002-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-002-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-003-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-003-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-003-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-003-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-004-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-004-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-004-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-004-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-005-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-005-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-005-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-005-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-006-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-006-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-006-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-006-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-007-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-007-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-007-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-007-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-008-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-008-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-008-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-008-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-009-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-009-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-009-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-009-CONTINUE, MI-NORMAL-010-ACCEPT, MI-NORMAL-010-GATE, MI-FL2-S1-010-ACCEPT, MI-FL2-S2-010-CONTINUE | SELECTED: Turn producer/campaign/integration/practical results into exactly one authoritative semantic verdict or one classified correction route. This module owns decisions; it performs no product repair itself. This product's exact identities, trust, real native/service proof and recovery require the configured occurrences; no extra runner or release ceremony. |

Turn producer/campaign/integration/practical results into exactly one authoritative semantic verdict
or one classified correction route. This module owns decisions; it performs no product repair itself.

## Public interface and compatibility boundary

complete evidence pool + gate criteria -> ROOT verdict, preserved/invalidated credit and exact correction card. Concrete values and return targets belong to the owning step's configured instance. Private changes do not alter public semantics; update actual consumers for a real contract change.

## Rules, process, recipe actions, and allowed variations

P01–P15 own shared rules. Semantic choices in recipe verbs remain ROOT decisions; test_root only authors declared lane-local contracts. Workers mechanically execute supplied choices and return any missing meaning. Reviewer findings remain open within assigned boundaries. Each governing card cites all actions; member cards cite only their ordered subset. Parameters may select bounded source/test paths and optional profiles, never weaken acceptance or change authority.

| Order | Recipe action ID | Project-specific action/process | Allowed parameterization | Decision owner |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | M05-A1 | Validate required result shape without treating shape validity as product success. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 2 | M05-A2 | Pool and deduplicate findings once; retain source, severity claim, violated requirement/invariant, reproducible results, and disagreements. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 3 | M05-A3 | Classify every follow-up as `production/material`, `strict test-only`, or `administrative/support`. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 4 | M05-A4 | For every test/support fault, issue `PRODUCT_INVALIDATING`, `NONBLOCKING_TEST_ERROR`, or `INDETERMINATE` with the product checks/observations and affected claims. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 5 | M05-A5 | Decide each finding against realistic product behavior, R25 and the finding-admissibility rule. Classify genuine but acceptance-irrelevant gaps as `REJECT-OUT-OF-SCOPE`; retain truthful nonblocking notes in Section 14, admit no repair tranche for them, and continue satisfied edges. Drop gold-plating to Section 14. Passing tests never relax a demonstrated required violation. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 6 | M05-A6 | Compute invalidated and preserved credit by dependency, not whole-document change alone. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 7 | M05-A7 | If admitted material findings remain, partition only by incompatible owner, source context, or acceptance criterion, then send each complete compatible group once to the same M02 logical instance and workflow role, reusing or handing off its invocation under the universal contract. Do not rerun assurance while a compatible group from the current pool remains open. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 8 | M05-A8 | If strict test-only eligibility is proved, return once to the same M03 logical role/task, reusing or handing off its invocation under the universal contract. Run the deterministic eligibility check and authorize exactly the known failed-ID rerun. If the correction or rerun fails, return to M05-A3 classification; never infer a production/material route from fast-lane failure. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 9 | M05-A9 | If the issue is administrative and facts remain decidable, correct/reconstruct from existing result only when an exact consumer still requires it and correction is cheaper than recording it; otherwise record it and take every independently satisfied successor without product/test rerun. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |
| 10 | M05-A10 | Issue exactly one verdict: `ACCEPTED`, `ACCEPT-WITHIN-TOLERANCE` only for a predeclared non-required tolerance (never a required criterion), `CONTINUE`, or `INCOMPLETE`; permit `CONTINUE` only for a failed/genuinely undecidable required product criterion. | owning instance exact paths, proof goals, predecessor output and selected scope | root |

- Material return: M05 -> same M02 logical role/task -> affected M04 -> same M05; reuse or hand off
  the invocation under the universal contract.
- Strict test-only return: M05 -> same M03 logical role/task -> exact affected check path -> same M05;
  reuse or hand off the invocation under the universal contract.
- Failed strict test-only correction/rerun: return to M05 classification. Enter M02 only when separate
  product results satisfy R12/R24; otherwise mark only the affected claim unavailable or
  `INDETERMINATE` and advance all nonconsumers.
- Administrative correction, when still consumed: remain in the originating result lane, resume at
  the first unresolved artifact action, validate, then return to the same M05. Otherwise record and advance.
- Accepted work unlocks only declared graph successors and makes the logical task terminal; every
  invocation associated with it is then terminal or retired under the plan's lifecycle rule.
- Every correction, repeat, or integration successor is activated only by a separately authored card
  containing the complete revised problem/result/scope/proof/pitfall/acceptance contract. ROOT authors
  terminal, cross-lane, and integration successors; an authorized lane sub-orchestrator may author only
  its declared local successor. An M05 verdict or route never authorizes a worker to continue on its own.
- A non-product fault with independently decisive product results cannot select `CONTINUE` or
  `INCOMPLETE`, cannot enter M02, and cannot hold a successor that does not consume that fault.

## Configured module instances

### MI-NORMAL-001-ACCEPT - M05: Freeze the builder and qualify the development boundary / NORMAL / ACCEPT

#### Purpose

ROOT owns classification, requirement-versus-oracle adjudication, credit, acceptance and next cards. The routine member validates result shape and mechanically deduplicates identified facts only, preserving disagreements; it never makes a verdict. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-001; REQ-001, REQ-027, REQ-028, REQ-029; STEP-001 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required adjudication, acceptance, and correction routing
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-001-ACCEPT-POOL=routine

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-001-CHECK.typed output satisfying M05 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-001-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records a code/test-tip accept-or-return decision from available local checks and review, authorizing separate integration only. Later native/service/final product gates are still open. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. #### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-001-CONTINUE.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`development/dogfood/`, `development/test-tools/`, disposable
`development/test-runs/` and `development/evidence/`; deliberately staged candidate
configuration/test assets in `P` only. Reference source remains unchanged. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–2 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-NORMAL-002-GATE - M05: Establish product contracts, privacy and durable local evidence / NORMAL / GATE

#### Purpose

ROOT owns classification, requirement-versus-oracle adjudication, credit, acceptance and next cards. The routine member validates result shape and mechanically deduplicates identified facts only, preserving disagreements; it never makes a verdict. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-002; REQ-002, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-019, REQ-021; STEP-002 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required adjudication, acceptance, and correction routing
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-002-GATE-POOL=routine

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-002-INTEGRATE.typed output satisfying M05 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

MI-NORMAL-002-INTEGRATE.typed output satisfying M05 public input; REQ-002, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-019, REQ-021; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Use this step's normal BUILD domain brief and root Section3 observations; this is a reference, not permission to infer new requirements.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-002-GATE.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/pyproject.toml`, product lock, `P/src/memory_harness/{contracts,config,evidence,privacy}/`,
`P/tests/{contracts,privacy,evidence}/`, product setup documentation and
`P/harness/REUSE_MANIFEST.md`. ROOT owns shared exports and common test fixtures. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-002-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/pyproject.toml`, product lock, `P/src/memory_harness/{contracts,config,evidence,privacy}/`,
`P/tests/{contracts,privacy,evidence}/`, product setup documentation and
`P/harness/REUSE_MANIFEST.md`. ROOT owns shared exports and common test fixtures. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records acceptance or classified return for the bounded repair objective and retained smoke/review; acceptance authorizes separate integration, not a full-product PASS. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

template, `P/tests/integration/`, candidate `P/harness/orchestrator_harness/`
bootstrap/resume/config/provider seams, and assigned synthetic service resources.
These candidate changes never modify the frozen builder. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records a code/test-tip accept-or-return decision from available local checks and review, authorizing separate integration only. Later native/service/final product gates are still open. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-003-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

minimum implementations in `P/src/memory_harness/`, a minimal reviewed
template, `P/tests/integration/`, candidate `P/harness/orchestrator_harness/`
bootstrap/resume/config/provider seams, and assigned synthetic service resources.
These candidate changes never modify the frozen builder. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records acceptance or classified return for the bounded repair objective and retained smoke/review; acceptance authorizes separate integration, not a full-product PASS. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-003-CONTINUE - M05: Prove the thin end-to-end product slice / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / CONTINUE

ROOT owns classification, requirement-versus-oracle adjudication, credit, acceptance and next cards. template, `P/tests/integration/`, candidate `P/harness/orchestrator_harness/`
bootstrap/resume/config/provider seams, and assigned synthetic service resources.
These candidate changes never modify the frozen builder. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–5 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-004-CONTINUE.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; only disjoint adapter writes overlap. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/experience/`, `P/tests/experience/`, focused
EverOS extensions/tests only under `P/harness/vendor/everos/`. No shared schema,
Atlas module, common fixtures or package-manifest edits without ROOT reassignment. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-005-GATE.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; only disjoint adapter writes overlap. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/procedures/`, `P/src/memory_harness/atlas/`,
`P/tests/{procedures,atlas}/`, scoped index/bootstrap definitions and operator
documentation. Any vendored MongoDB changes stay in its candidate copy and get
their own affected upstream tests; none is presumed necessary. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S1-005-ACCEPT - M05: Complete Atlas procedures and distributed lifecycle / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_1 / ACCEPT

#### Purpose

ROOT owns classification, requirement-versus-oracle adjudication, credit, acceptance and next cards. The routine member validates result shape and mechanically deduplicates identified facts only, preserving disagreements; it never makes a verdict. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained.

#### Coverage

DEL-005; REQ-005, REQ-006, REQ-007, REQ-008, REQ-009, REQ-010, REQ-013; STEP-005 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_1.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-006-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{search,templates,apc}/`, five reviewed template
assets, `P/tests/{search,templates,apc}/`; candidate harness delegation adapter
only through ROOT-owned seam changes. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records a code/test-tip accept-or-return decision from available local checks and review, authorizing separate integration only. Later native/service/final product gates are still open. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-006-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{search,templates,apc}/`, five reviewed template
assets, `P/tests/{search,templates,apc}/`; candidate harness delegation adapter
only through ROOT-owned seam changes. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records acceptance or classified return for the bounded repair objective and retained smoke/review; acceptance authorizes separate integration, not a full-product PASS. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

ROOT owns classification, requirement-versus-oracle adjudication, credit, acceptance and next cards. The routine member validates result shape and mechanically deduplicates identified facts only, preserving disagreements; it never makes a verdict. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Coverage

DEL-007; REQ-001, REQ-003, REQ-006, REQ-011, REQ-016, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-021, REQ-024; STEP-007 NORMAL.

#### Selection basis

Required adjudication, acceptance, and correction routing
 boundary; Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence.

#### Owner and roles

root owns bounded instance mechanics; root retains global decisions. Dispatch inventory: CARD-NORMAL-007-ACCEPT-POOL=routine

#### Preconditions

MI-NORMAL-007-CHECK.typed output satisfying M05 public input is verified; exact role resolution and scope authorization precede launch.

MI-NORMAL-007-CHECK.typed output satisfying M05 public input; REQ-001, REQ-003, REQ-006, REQ-011, REQ-016, REQ-017, REQ-018, REQ-021, REQ-024; current candidate and consumed state/checkpoints. Use this step's normal BUILD domain brief and root Section3 observations; this is a reference, not permission to infer new requirements.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-007-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{context,composition}/`, candidate harness
bootstrap/resume/setup/provider payloads, product ROOT provider configuration,
`P/tests/{context,composition,features,security}/`. Shared config edits are serialized. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records a code/test-tip accept-or-return decision from available local checks and review, authorizing separate integration only. Later native/service/final product gates are still open. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

`P/tests/{context,composition,features,security}/`. Shared config edits are serialized. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. #### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-007-CONTINUE.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{context,composition}/`, candidate harness
bootstrap/resume/setup/provider payloads, product ROOT provider configuration,
`P/tests/{context,composition,features,security}/`. Shared config edits are serialized. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S2-008-CONTINUE.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

`P/src/memory_harness/{evidence,reconcile,usage,snapshots,cli}/`,
`P/tests/{recovery,usage,snapshots,cli}/`, product operator docs. Serial ownership
of evidence code previously established by STEP-002. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

2–4 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-009-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

disposable fixture repositories and evidence under the wave root;
no product source changes during collection. Product defects return to their
outer implementation owner and a newly pinned candidate wave. Additionally P/src/memory_harness/cli/demo.py and P/tests/native implement the required synthetic demo entrypoint, not a benchmark runner. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records acceptance or classified return for the bounded repair objective and retained smoke/review; acceptance authorizes separate integration, not a full-product PASS. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

1–3 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-NORMAL-010-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

product README/runbook, `P/harness/REUSE_MANIFEST.md`, product release
evidence references, `development/HANDOFF.md`. No public release/push is implied. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records a code/test-tip accept-or-return decision from available local checks and review, authorizing separate integration only. Later native/service/final product gates are still open. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; ROOT decision precedes next module dispatch. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–1 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Normal complete stage work and its independently selected evidence. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

#### Outputs and results

MI-FL2-S1-010-ACCEPT.ROOT verdict and credit map; native result and unique member output paths; factual reports are not acceptance.

#### Concurrency and isolation

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

product README/runbook, `P/harness/REUSE_MANIFEST.md`, product release
evidence references, `development/HANDOFF.md`. No public release/push is implied. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records acceptance or classified return for the bounded repair objective and retained smoke/review; acceptance authorizes separate integration, not a full-product PASS. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

After acceptance and separate integration, exit only to ROOT's selected current progress bound through its Series 2 entry. P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; accepted integrated repaired output exits to current-bound Series 2 only. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–1 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Outbound scoped repair only. Required smoke is changed-source compile plus the exact motivating tests from ROOT's diagnosed correction card. No broad deterministic campaign; independent review and separate accepted integration retained. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.

### MI-FL2-S2-010-CONTINUE - M05: Development closeout and deferral boundary / FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2 / CONTINUE

#### Purpose

ROOT owns classification, requirement-versus-oracle adjudication, credit, acceptance and next cards. The routine member validates result shape and mechanically deduplicates identified facts only, preserving disagreements; it never makes a verdict. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision.

#### Coverage

DEL-010; REQ-029, REQ-031; STEP-010 FAST_LANE_V2_SERIES_2.

Required adjudication, acceptance, and correction routing

Source frozen during collection. Separate native WORKTREE/member IDs and result/cache/temp roots; one writer at shared seams. M04/M07 independent paths launch before waiting; Section10 shared budget, no added scheduler.

#### Resources and side effects

product README/runbook, `P/harness/REUSE_MANIFEST.md`, product release
evidence references, `development/HANDOFF.md`. No public release/push is implied. Credentials remain protected configuration only. No live effects in M08; M09 exact allowed synthetic targets. Native resource IDs recorded/read back before cleanup; no broad deletion. Sessions unbounded, finite policy per P02.

#### Checks and acceptance

ROOT records accept-or-classified-return against this step's explicit BUILD gate slice using valid complete accumulated evidence. A required failed or unproved criterion prevents dependent product acceptance; unrelated ready consumers advance. Read the complete predecessor pool, actual source/state identities and required evidence; validate report schema/joins without rerunning commands. ROOT alone compares the explicit acceptance slice and classifies findings; a member only assembles facts. Stage product criteria remain the normal BUILD brief's explicit gate slice and root Section3; completion of this module is not automatic product acceptance.

#### Failure and exception routes

ROOT applies P07 owner routing from the complete pool: material product failure returns to the declared originating production M02 owner, strict test-only to that asset's M03 owner under P08, and operational/report fault stays with its factual producer under P09. The observing step does not gain write authority; P04 returns accepted repairs to the current progress bound, and P15 contains only observed live harm.

#### Prior results and change effects

P04 external-credit exit: Series2 REMAIN reconciles local check/evidence credit and records any affected/uncertain/uncredited required live unit. Such a result terminates this fast-path attempt at ROOT for normal material/evidence classification; do not dispatch this Series2 CONTINUE or claim completed Series2 for that event. ROOT separately selects the normal affected-evidence continuation using only the recorded observation owner's existing M08 READY/M09 REAL pair: MI-NORMAL-001-READY and MI-NORMAL-001-REAL for baseline; MI-NORMAL-003-READY and MI-NORMAL-003-REAL for thin slice/APC A; MI-NORMAL-004-READY and MI-NORMAL-004-REAL for EverOS; MI-NORMAL-005-READY and MI-NORMAL-005-REAL for Atlas; MI-NORMAL-007-READY and MI-NORMAL-007-REAL for provider/context; MI-NORMAL-009-READY and MI-NORMAL-009-REAL for integrated/APC B/cleanup. Use the current pinned candidate, explicit attempt authority, only invalid required coordinates, and unchanged readiness credit. Normal continuation returns to the current progress bound's MI-NORMAL-NNN-GATE (NNN is this STEP number), with MI-NORMAL-010-ASSURE's affected final audit first when bound010. It never follows the observation origin's successor. No BUILD/ASSETS replay, unaffected role sweep, or full safeguard repeat. Already accepted repairs/local PASS remain credited. These normal instances are outside, not hidden within, the configured Series2 path. For remaining local-only reconciliation, REMAIN then CONTINUE completes the configured distinct Series2 path normally. M04/M07 do not launch live attempts; unavailable required live evidence blocks only its dependent claim.

Receive every accepted Series 1 exit; use the input invalidation map to preserve unaffected PASS. Start the earliest required remaining unit, then normal successor rules. P04 maps source/schema/config/runner/fixtures and actual external target state; keep historical results and unchanged PASS, rerun only earliest affected/unresolved set; no whole-plan diff invalidation.

#### Repeat, join, and terminal behavior

P02/P04/P07; complete pool, one join; join every accepted repair exit then remaining checks and normal completion. No worker self-dispatch or unbounded replacements.

#### Cost and critical-path effect

0.5–1 engineering days for owning stage, not per instance. This M05 uses 1 terminal worker assignments only when active; local commands estimated5–120s, native scenario2–10min, service30–300s. Inbound reconciliation only: join accepted exits, recalculate consumed inputs, preserve unaffected PASS and execute only remaining failed/unresolved/affected/uncertain/uncredited units; normal successors after ROOT decision. Reuse eliminates only work with unchanged inputs, no measured savings claimed.
